TRUSTWORTHINESS ASSESSMENT
======================================================================
  ForgetScore  F = 1 - mean_leak(forget)        = 0.580   (1.0 = fully forgotten)
  UtilityScore U = mean_knowledge(retain)       = 0.640   (1.0 = fully preserved)
  Trust        T = 2FU / (F + U)                = 0.609   (harmonic mean)

  Verbatim memorization (ROUGE-L, forget set):  0.983 -> 0.586
